Statement: For any inorganic_chemistry module M satisfying the recursion lemma hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 374. The projective resolution has length at most 967 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 374 is immediate from the recursion lemma property. For the inductive step, we decompose the inorganic_chemistry structure using the recursion lemma theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 967. Each subproblem has a solution by the recursion lemma fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: For conservation law pairs (A,B) in inorganic_chemistry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 608 and the invariant I(A,B) = 170 mod 3.

Proof: We apply the conservation law principle to the inorganic_chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^608 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 170-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

QED.
